2.1.4. van der Waals Complexes

complexes.As expected,the n(HF)of Ne(cid:1)HF [269] and n(HCl) of Ne–HCl [270]
areshiftedonlyslightly(þ0.4722andþ3024cm (cid:1)1,respectively)fromtheirfrequen-

reported[271–275].IRspectraofvanderWaalscomplexessuchasCO(cid:1)CH [276],

3
the fundamental frequencies of XH -type molecules. Several bands marked by an
3

3
betweenthemissmall,themoleculemayresonatebetweenthetwostructures.Asa
result, each vibrational level splits into two levels (positive and negative) [560].
Transitions between levels of different signs are allowed in the infrared spectrum,
whereasthosebetweenlevelsofthesamesignareallowedintheRamanspectrum.The
transitionbetweenthetwolevelsatv¼0isalsoobservedinthemicrowaveregion
(~n¼0:79cm(cid:1)1).
